Section 2

Chapter 5

  • Mahbubur Rahman
    North American University, USA
  • Mustafa Duran
    North American University, USA

ABSTRACT

The authors emphasize the application scope of the deep learning (DL) technologies in the instructional analysis, design, development, implementation, and evaluation of the educational tools that can enhance the 21st-century modes and models of learning and instruction. The latest trend in the remote learning systems opens a wonderful opportunity for the DL technologies to be integrated with these systems that can impact the way of learning, teaching, design, and development of such systems. The DL technologies provide the data driven decisions and analytical outcomes that can be integrated with the educational technologies. The existing educational technologies and remote learning systems lack in such integrated DL services that can impact the overall education learning systems of the 21st century. The learners and instructors can also benefit from such DL-integrated educational tools. The authors expand further details on the application and implication of the DL services mentioned above in the chapter.

INTRODUCTION

The modes and models of learning and instruction in the 21st century has entered the remote learning era around a decade ago. As a result, it brings the challenge of successful integration, implementation of the remote learning into the existing one and introduces the concept of eLearning management system. On the other hand, the DL is impacting every aspect of the human lives from the beginning of the 21st century. The eLearning systems can be no such exceptions to the impact. As a result, the DL backed ADDIE can add values to the modes and models of the learning and instruction in the 21st century.

The ADDIE can play a significant role in the 21st century modes and models of learning and instruction. The instructors and the students are the key audiences in this model of learning and instruction. Hence, the authors will advance the analysis considering the effect of the ADDIE on them alongside the challenges involved, while implementing the ADDIE efficiently here. The following sections and subsections highlight and explain the ADDIE aided instruction and learning modes and models, their impacts, applications etc.

BACKGROUND

There are some existing online learning services as the Moodle, Jenzabar, Blackboard etc. (Community M., 2021), (Community J., 2021), (Community B., 2021). These services provide the basic online learning management services for the institution, instructors, and students (e.g., Fig 1, 2). The instructors can upload the course materials, recorded class sessions, discussion topics etc. The students can also view the course materials, recorded sessions. As a result, these online learning services become an integral part of the 21st century remote learning model. Moreover, these eLearning management systems provide some analytical services associated with the learners’ enrollment, engagement, retention rate etc. As for example, the florida southern college manages recruitment and retention data with the Jenzabar analytics (Community J., 2021). The Blackboard analytics provide deep insights for various data analytics and predictions to increase effectiveness in enrollment management, recruitment and retention, instructional design and identification of at-risk students (Community B., 2021).

However, these systems lack in the ADDIE aided online learning modes and models and that is where the motivation behind incorporating such ADDIE aided services originate from. There are several studies, that support such incorporation as suggested by (Muniasamy & Alasiry, 2020), (Engelbrecht, 2003), (Martens, Gulikers, & Bastiaens, 2004). Inspired by the studies and applications and implications of the DL, the authors are interested to provide the guidelines for incorporating such services in this chapter.

The ADDIE will incorporate the DL services in the existing remote learning systems. The DL services should be consistent with the modes and models of the learning systems for better impact on the system. Today’s DL technologies have applications on almost all aspects of human lives (Deng & Yu, 2014) (Zhou, Zhang, Liu, Qiu, & He, 2019) (Mahdavifar & Ghorbani, 2019) (Zhu, Xie, Han, & Guo, 2020). The DL technologies can help to advance the data driven analysis more efficiently than the existing one by the help of different DL technologies as the convolutional neural network (e.g., CNN), recurrent neural network (e.g., RNN) etc. The data driven analysis can impact the decisions associated with the predicting weather, supply-chain relationship, stock price, disease, translating language etc., almost all aspects of human lives!

Figure 1. Course overview page from the Moodle

However, the authors focus the impact of the DL technologies on the ADDIE here as the impact, importance become imminent suggested by several studies (Ahad, Tripathi, & Agarwal, 2018) (Paris & Paris, 2001) (Doleck, Lemay, Basnet, & Bazelais, 2019). The characteristic of all-aspects impact of the DL technologies can help to contribute to the ADDIE as well. As a result, the ADDIE can be more beneficial to the eLearning management systems for providing better analytical services. The rest of the chapters introduce the concepts of DL integrated ADDIE and its overall impact on the 21st century modes and models of learning and instruction.

Figure 2. Course management page from the Jenzabar eLearning system

ADDIE AIDED INSTRUCTION

The modes and models of the instruction has been changing at the beginning of the 21st century by the advancement of internet technology (Agarwal & Day, 2020) (Banica, 2017) (Hirschheim, 2005). The remote access service provided by this technology allows easy, 24/7 access to the eLearning system. The DL backed services on top of this remote access can have enough impact on the system. The authors define the ADDIE aided instruction modes and models as the instructions governed, supported by different DL backed services included in the ADDIE. The services included in the ADDIE for the instruction can be enhanced search results for different topics, student performance management, data security management, that can impact the overall instruction modes and models as follows.

  • • Enhanced search result by the ADDIE: Everyone searches for specific information on the internet now-a-days. The instructors are not exception to this. They need to search and summarize the search result for the analysis, discussion, presentation etc. The ADDIE can provide enhanced search result for any course related topic by incorporating the state-of-art deep learning backed search engines in the online learning management system (e.g., Fig 1). This search engine can search in different websites for a given topic to find the application, implication of the given topic in different resources, websites, domains etc. As a result, the instructors along with the students can have up-to-date information, knowledge of the same topic in different domains.
Figure 3. The CGI generated search topic option integrated with the existing Jenzabar eLearning system can search the topic associated with different domains by the state-of-art deep learning-based searching algorithms

The technologies involved in the multidimensional search topic includes the web crawler, that can scan or crawl through the web pages to create an index of the data, it is set to look for (NT, 2021). Finally, the DL models on top of the indices can recommend the similarity of the search topic in different domains (Rahman, 2020). As a result, the instructors can search and receive results for the topic of interest in different web pages and summarize the result on the course portal for the students. It is very interesting and beneficial for the students as they can understand the subject matters from different perspectives. As for example, the students from the department of the computer science can find the application, implications of different programming languages in different domains. Similarly, the students from the department of the language can learn different languages simply looking at the search results translated into the respective languages.

  • • Enhanced student performance management by the ADDIE: The existing eLearning systems provide necessary course works (e.g., assignments, quizzes, discussions etc.) related with the courses. Some students perform better while others may not in the courses. Depending on the performance of the students in the courses, the students can be classified as outstanding or at-risk as well. The ADDIE can analyze the performance of the students by applying state-of-art DL classifier (Zhang S. a., February 2019) technology to classify the students in different classes and recommend corresponding actions (e.g., tutor service, deadline extension for the course works etc.) to the responsible personnel. As a result, the ADDIE can provide better student performance management system, that can impact the student success rate in the courses.

The DL technologies can be integrated with the recommendation systems, that can provide specific recommendation for specific students while analyzing their performances (Mu, 2018). The remote learning systems can provide the data related with the performance of the students to the ADDIE to design the recommendation systems. The DL backed performance monitoring systems have been gaining popularity in other disciplines as well (Sundaravadivel, Kesavan, Kesavan, Mohanty, & Kougianos, 2018) (Yolcu, Oztel, & Bunyak, 2019). Hence, it can impact on the 21st century modes and models of the learning.

The instructors need to spend sometimes to grade the course works. It requires significant amount of time. The ADDIE can provide recommended grade for the submitted assignment by incorporating the natural language processing (e.g., NLP) service in the system. The NLP can be trained on the given rubrics for grading the assignments and then incorporated in the system to enhance the grading. It can provide better grade for the correct submission than the incorrect one. On the other hand, each institution has policies regarding the plagiarism. The ADDIE can provide the instructors DL backed plagiarism services (e.g. (Community T., 2021)) to handle such plagiarism related policies efficiently. As a result, the ADDIE can play an important role in the institutional policies, effective plans.

The instructors, students with the disabilities can get benefit from the services provided by the ADDIE as discussed above. The existing DL services can be customized to provide instructional assistance to the disabled individuals. As a result, the ADDIE can play important role for determining the institutional policies of the disability.

  • • Enhanced data security management by the ADDIE: The security breach can occur and create threats for the eLearning management systems as well. The ADDIE can analyze the existing network data traffics and detect possible cyber-attacks and take immediate necessary action. Today’s deep learning algorithms can provide inferential outcome, causal effects, while analyzing the data (Ke & Xie, 2009) (Batmaz, Yurekli, Bilge, & Kaleli, 2019). This technology can be integrated with the eLearning management system to analyze the network traffic, access and then, detect possible intruder, hacker, denial of service attack (Ferrag, Maglaras, Moschoyiannis, & Janicke, 2020) (Berman, Buczak, Chavis, & Corbett, 2019). As a result, the existing data in the system can be more secured.

The data is the center of analysis in the DL techniques. However, the data needs to be secured for various reasons as reducing the unprivileged accesses, cyberattacks, well-being of the organizations etc. The remote access feature of the 21st century modes and models of the learning also bring the security concerns. However, the DL backed security management system can get rid of such concerns.

The organizations suffer from multimillion to multibillion of dollars because of the security breach (Obderbeck, 2016). The remote learning system is not an exception to this cyberattack. There have been several attempts of cyberattacks in the educational institutions for the last several years (e.g., Fig 4). The attackers steal the identity of the employer (e.g., (Gandhi, et al., 2011), (Kwon, Liu, & Hwang, 2013)) of the institutions and then attempt to attack. The 21st century mode and models of learning must incorporate the remote learning feature to provide an easy access of online learning to the respected professionals. The DL backed cyber security measures of the ADDIE can ensure such secured access, data management of the eLearning systems. The cybersecurity measures can analyze the traffic flow and identify potential threats there. The same measures can be used to identify potential attempts on the stealing of the identity of the educational personnel, students etc. The identity management service can be integrated with the DL backed identity service that can provide more secured access to the data (Arsenovic, Sladojevic, Anderla, & Stefanovic, 2017). As a result, the ADDIE can help to reduce the management cost associated with the security of the data.

Figure 4. Recent cyberattack examples on a system. The attackers provided malicious links in the suspicious email.

ADDIE Aided Learning

The instructors and the students are one of the two most important participants in the eLearning system. The authors have explained the impact of the ADDIE aided instruction on the participants above. The impact can be extended on the learning part as well. The authors define the ADDIE aided learning modes and models as the method, tools of learning that are governed, managed, supported by different services included in the ADDIE. It can be the enhanced search results associated with the students’ course topics, digital learning, mentoring, career assistances, that can improve the overall learning outcome for the students along with the career success. The authors have addressed the ADDIE aided learning impact, benefits in the following subsections.

  • • Digital learning assistance by the ADDIE: The digital learning assistance (e.g., DLA) can provide the students up-to-date information related with the course topics, homeworks, assignments. The latest information received, processed by the DLA at the student’s end can be organized by the DL backed searching services at the instructor’s end. The students can understand the core concepts along with the application of the concepts in different domains from the searching service provided by the DLA.

The DLA can have a visual representation on the eLearning system (e.g., Fig 5) as the Microsoft word document provided such assistance some years ago. Such visuals can not only provide the students course related helps but also provide psychological strength at the difficult times. The DLA ensures 24/7 assistance for the students. Amazon, Google, Microsoft have been building the digital assistants for several years to provide such 24/7 assistance to the customers (Maedche, et al., 2019). The authors are considering similar types of DLA for the 21st century modes and models of learning for the learners here, that can be integral part of the students’ day-to-day learning activities.

Figure 5. The visual representation of the DLA on the Jenzabar eLearning management system

Some learners require continuous assistance. However, it is not possible for the mentors. The DLA can play a vital role providing continuous assistance to the respective students. While assisting, the DLA can identify at-risk students and recommend corresponding measures to succeed to the students. In this way, the DLA can provide feedback related to the performance of the students to the enhanced student performance management system at the instructors’ end. The importance of the DLA has been recommended in several studies as (Brock & Smith, 2007), (Myles, Ferguson, & Hagiwara, 2007) (Chatterley & Chojecki, 2010).

  • • Digital mentoring assistance by the ADDIE: The digital mentoring assistance (DMA) can provide the students continuous mentoring service, that includes easy to understand summary of difficult concepts, visual representations, simulations of process, course contents translated into the students’ own language. The DL is bringing revolution in the natural language processing (e.g., NLP) (T. Young, 2018) (D. W. Otter, 2021). Similar types of DL backed NLP services can be integrated with the ADDIE for mentoring assistance in the student’s own language (e.g., Fig 6). The DMA can provide 24/7 mentoring assistance to the students. As a result, this can improve the overall performance of the students.
Figure 6. The existing transcript service provided by the Moodle learning system. The ADDIE can provide NLP service on top of that to make the course contents available to the requested language.

The language can be an obstacle to the modes and models of the learning at 21st century. Some learners face problems understanding the content because of deficiency of the language on which the content is written. However, the NLP service integrated with the ADDIE can remove such obstacles, while translating the content to the respective languages.

The continuous mentoring service by the DMA requires continuous analysis with the student’s performance and necessary recommendations. The enhanced student performance management by the ADDIE at the instructors’ end can provide at-risk students’ information to the DMA at the students’ end. Then the DMA can recommend mentoring schemes depending on the performance of the student. The DMA can also follow the guidelines for success in the academic life in different resources and then, recommend such guidelines to the students.

  • • Digital career assistance by the ADDIE: A successful career requires a successful training, mentoring. Hence, the 21st century modes and models of learning should have such career mentoring, assistance services. The digital career assistance (e.g., DCA) can provide 24/7 career related information, guidelines to the students. Each student has specific career goal. The DCA can be configured in such a way that it can provide student specific career objectives, suggestions, recommendations etc. The instructors can also get information on the demanding careers from the DCA and then design the course curriculum to meet the demand.

The DCA can be connected to the job portals (Job Search | Indeed, 2021) (LinkedIn, 2021) so that immediate notifications can be sent out to the students matching their interests, skills. The student career service in the institution can work in parallel with the DCA to provide more enhanced career assistance to the students.

The resume plays a significant role in successful job interviews. The DCA can have resume critique service integrated such that the students can receive necessary recommendations, critiques to update their resumes. The DL backed services in the DCA can classify good or bad resumes and then provide necessary recommendations to have a good resume (Cocarascu & Toni, 2018) (Sun & Vasarhelyi, 2018).

The DL based video analysis is an emerging field (Nishani & Çiço, 2017) and by incorporating such services, the ADDIE can be more beneficial to the future career endeavor of the students. The students can prepare recorded mock interview sessions and then upload the session to the DCA. The DCA can provide feedbacks on the recorded video sessions of the mock interviews by the students. The students can improve their interview skills depending on the feedback from the DCA. In this way, the ADDIE can contribute to the career advancement of the students in every other way.

CHALLENGES ASSOCIATED WITH THE ADDIE

The ADDIE can provide invaluable services to the online learning platform as it has been explained above. The instructors, students and other respected professionals can benefit from such services. The implementation, maintenance of the ADDIE can create many opportunities in the respected fields contributing to the employment sectors as well. The educational personnel can work in the state-of-the-art learning technology system. The learners can also learn the application of such technologies in the 21st century modes and models of learning.

However, it has challenges as well. The challenges include proper implementation, management of the ADDIE. The implementation of the ADDIE requires expertise in the DL, software, hardware technologies. The DL requires enough data to be trained on. This data requires proper management by the database server, data center etc. The management of such services also requires expertise in the DL, cloud related technologies (Shi, 2021) (Zhang, Yan, Chen, & Li, 2018) (Bisong, 2019). Finally, the corresponding organizations need to allocate necessary funding for the development, deployment and maintenance of the ADDIE based eLearning management systems. The authors provided more in-depth analysis of the challenges and respective measures to resolve as below.

  • • Challenges associated with the enhanced search and DLA: The enhanced search service requires multiple data sources connected, processed. This is a complex process that requires searching engine design, development, and deployment in the ADDIE. Additionally, the cost associated with the incorporation of commercial web crawlers can limit the searching analysis (Mirtaheri, et al., 2014).

On the other hand, the DLA, and other associated agents (e.g., DMA, DCA etc.) receive inputs from the enhanced search engine and then display this to the learners. As a result, the enhanced search engine needs to have multiple connectivity features included to ensure concurrent access by the instructors, learners. All these challenges can be overcome by proper planning, accurate implementation, maintenance of the DL backed search engines following the available guidelines. The respected institutions can have discussions with the information technology department on the proper implementation, maintenance of such DL backed services in their systems.

  • • Challenges associated with the enhanced student performance management by the ADDIE: The enhanced student performance management system requires enough data to provide student specific recommendation, plagiarism analysis (Najafabadi, et al., 2015). As a result, the ADDIE needs to process, manage student record efficiently to provide such recommendations. Additional data base server needs to be incorporated to maintain, process such analysis. It might require additional budget to maintain such servers.

On the contrary, there are open-source servers that can be used to manage the student performance (Ven K., 2012) (Ven & Verelst, 2011) and other recommended services. Once the ADDIE can access to the student data in the server, then it can analyze and provide student specific recommendations. The DL services incorporated in the ADDIE can provide such recommendations.

  • • Challenges associated with the enhanced data security management by the ADDIE: It is challenging to make the distributed system secured (Y Xin, 2018). The potential hackers, intruders can attempt to steal the identity of the students along with their financial credentials. However, appropriate security measures implemented in the eLearning system can reduce such cyberattacks. The security measures include strong password creations, multifactor authentication (Ometov, et al., 2018), other necessary security service integration in the eLearning system.

The ADDIE services are data driven. The data requires protection against cyberattacks. The cyberattacks can occur anytime. Hence, 24/7 security service is also required to manage the security of the data (Singh, Jeong, & Park, 2020). The existing network, system administrators can be trained on the DL backed security services to integrate, manage such services in the eLearning system.

  • • Challenges associated with the digital assistance by the ADDIE: The authors explained different types of digital assistance by the ADDIE above. The most challenging part of such digital assistance is its acceptability to the target audience. The learners are used to receive assistances from the human. The digital assistance by the ADDIE is a new concept. The concept can be made popular to the learners by providing 24/7 continuous efficient requested service. The educational professionals should receive proper guidelines on how to operate such digital assistances efficiently.

Another challenge might arise from the permission of using the data to provide the decisions from the ADDIE. Special protocols associated with the access privilege of the data might be required to be published and agreed by the interested personnel. This might require additional training, awareness in the respected individual. The multidimensional search engine by the ADDIE can be configured such that it can recommend relevant protocols, agreements for the audience to overcome such challenges.

CONCLUSION

The DL technologies have applications in different science, engineering, socio-economic domains. The authors proposed several ways of the integration of the DL technologies with the ADDIE and explained its applications, impact on the 21st century modes and models of learning and teaching here. There are challenges; however, there are significant outcome as well. The ADDIE can ensure easy, secured access to the remote learning system for all. As a result, it will help to get closer to the goal of education for all (UNESCO, 2020).

The modes and models of learning and teaching in the 21st century is advancing by the remote learning system. To keep up with such advancements, effective measures need to be taken. The ADDIE can be such effective measures that can help the respected professionals to be more effective in their fields. The authors provided in depth analysis on the implementation, application, challenges associated with the measures in this chapter. Following such measures, the incorporation of the ADDIE in the remote learning system can further enhance the 21st century modes and models of learning and instructions.
